    $storageTechnologySeen = @{}
    foreach ($domain in $domains) { $storageTechnologySeen[$domain.Name] = $false }

    foreach ($module in $backendModules) {
        $domain = $domains | Where-Object {
            $module.Path.StartsWith("$($_.Package)/")
        } | Select-Object -First 1
        if ($null -eq $domain) { continue }

        $insideAdapters = $module.Path.StartsWith("$($domain.Adapters)/")

        $lineNumber = 0
        foreach ($line in $module.Lines) {
            $lineNumber++
            foreach ($pattern in $storageTechnology) {
                if ($line -notmatch $pattern) { continue }

                if ($insideAdapters) {
                    $storageTechnologySeen[$domain.Name] = $true
                    break
                }

                $failures.Add(("Storage technology above the adapter layer in " +
                    "$($module.Path):${lineNumber}: $($line.Trim()). Move it into " +
                    "$($domain.Adapters) and speak domain records across the port.")) | Out-Null
                break
            }
        }
    }

    foreach ($domain in $domains) {
        if (-not $storageTechnologySeen[$domain.Name]) {
            $failures.Add(("The storage-technology check matched nothing inside " +
                "$($domain.Adapters), so its patterns may no longer match real " +
                "storage code. Update the check, do not delete it.")) | Out-Null
        }
    }

    # 3. The shipped configuration roots are not writable. Shipped canonical
    #    configuration is read-only at runtime, so no module that resolves a
    #    path inside a shipped root may also hold a write-capable call.
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $shippedCatalogRoot -PathType Container)) {
        $failures.Add("Missing shipped template configuration root: $shippedCatalogRoot") | Out-Null
    }
    elseif (-not (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $shippedCatalogRoot -Filter "*.yaml" -File)) {
        $failures.Add("$shippedCatalogRoot ships no template document") | Out-Null
    }

    # The shipped Site store exists and ships empty. M1 ships zero canonical
    # Sites, so a document appearing here would put a Site nobody configured
    # into the operator index and make the first-run empty state a lie.
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $shippedSiteRoot -PathType Container)) {
        $failures.Add("Missing shipped site configuration root: $shippedSiteRoot") | Out-Null
    }
    elseif (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $shippedSiteRoot -Filter "*.yaml" -File) {
        $failures.Add(("$shippedSiteRoot ships a site document. M1 ships zero " +
            "canonical sites: every site in the product is one a user created.")) | Out-Null
    }

    # The shipped scenario store exists and, unlike the Site store, does NOT
    # ship empty. T017 has no scenario creation flow, so without a tracked
    # definition the catalog would render an empty state on every fresh
    # checkout and the surface would have nothing to inspect. The asymmetry is
    # deliberate and is asserted rather than tolerated.
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $shippedScenarioRoot -PathType Container)) {
        $failures.Add("Missing shipped scenario configuration root: $shippedScenarioRoot") | Out-Null
    }
    elseif (-not (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $shippedScenarioRoot -Filter "*.yaml" -File)) {
        $failures.Add(("$shippedScenarioRoot ships no scenario definition. The " +
            "product has no scenario creation flow, so a fresh checkout would " +
            "have no scenario to inspect at all.")) | Out-Null
    }

    # Each named reference must be found somewhere, so that adding a shipped
    # root without wiring it in here cannot leave it unprotected.
    $shippedRootReferences = @(
        "site-templates",
        "SHIPPED_SITE_TEMPLATE_ROOT",
        "SHIPPED_SITE_ROOT",
        "SHIPPED_SCENARIO_ROOT"
    )
    $shippedRootReference = ($shippedRootReferences -join "|")
    $writeCapable = @(
        '\.write_text\(',
        '\.write_bytes\(',
        '\.mkdir\(',
        '\.touch\(',
        '\.unlink\(',
        '\.rmdir\(',
        '(?:^|\s)os\.(?:remove|unlink|replace|rename|makedirs|mkdir)\(',
        '(?:^|\s)shutil\.',
        '(?:^|\s)tempfile\.',
        'open\('
    )
    $shippedRootReferencesSeen = @{}
    foreach ($reference in $shippedRootReferences) { $shippedRootReferencesSeen[$reference] = $false }

    foreach ($module in $backendModules) {

codex
The main mismatches are concrete: event roles have no valid unit or conversion rule, one start-event channel cannot occupy two bindings, and the cold pack cannot be selected through the current Lab registries. I’m recording exact fixes for those seams, plus the Finding and assessment storage constraints.

-## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `GRID/METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. Exactly one such incoming room link per configured primary chamber probe and at most one primary probe per room; complete readiness requires exactly one per room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` or no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. This is contact, not equipment health. `bindings[].cadence` is the declared cadence of the frozen cold publication profile, `300` seconds for this profile, exposed as `cadence_seconds`; it is neither inferred from readings nor stored on a binding. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.
+
+**W2-02 channel closure.** Add `grid_power_event` / METER, EVENT `POWER_LOSS|POWER_RESTORED`, ONE, `event` unit. Replace the two impossible `backup_request` and `backup_result` channel bindings with **one** required `backup_start_event` / GENERATOR, EVENT `START_REQUEST|START_RESULT`, ONE; require both kinds in capability/readiness, while one registered `(generator-controller,start-event)` channel carries both. `grid_state` remains its separate `(grid-meter,state)` channel. `transfer_result`, `door_event` and `manual_temperature` keep separate channels. The W2-00 closed v4 table and W2-01 snapshot rows must name these exact roles; no channel may be bound twice. On cold panels replace status labels `Mapped`/`Not mapped` with `Configured`/`Needs a source`; use `Reporting`/`No report received` only when an actual selected evidence/contact read proves it. Keep panel titles fixed by the rendered-panel manifest.
+
+**W2-02 code seam closure.** Add cold model and publication identities to `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` `MODEL_PROFILES`/`PUBLICATION_PROFILES`, `host/execution_adapter.py` `EXECUTABLE_MODELS`, `host/lab_execution.py` model/reporting selection and its `_LIFECYCLE` validator, and `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py` `_MAPPING_BY_PUBLICATION_PROFILE`. The latter currently falls back to fuel mapping for an unknown profile: explicitly refuse an unknown cold/EV profile instead of falling back. Add the cold scenario state keys/scopes to the closed state-reference and execution registries and a separate cold pack/observation transform; `kernel/execute.py` currently assumes mini-grid/fuel model laws and does not acquire a new pack merely by receiving a `ModelSpec`. Use a typed pack dispatch boundary or extend generic laws with proof that existing mini-grid vectors are unchanged. `host/lab_execution.py` currently refuses non-measurement staging as V3; select the v4 stage/Batch V2 path by the frozen cold profile, including stable event IDs and pinned registered bindings, while preserving the V3 path. `profiles.py` owns publication cadence; Foundation declares availability/unit only. Freeze observation/profile/pack identity before Draft and include them in run identity. W2-02 may not call S2 with a synthetic unregistered mapping or use the W2-00 test-only injected basis.
+
+**W2-03 code seam closure.** The current `assessment_worker.py` is dispatch-only and `postgres_assessment_store.py` selects numeric readings; add a cold-specific typed evidence query/worker and adapter methods that select both v4 measurement and EVENT rows by accepted disposition decision time, tenant/Site, role, selected Foundation/binding and half-open source interval. Do not coerce event null values to readings. Reuse the existing `recompute_job` table under `domain='cold_chain'`, but give it a separate domain-specific lease and worker path; existing dispatch selection/job behavior remains unchanged. Extend `postgres_assessment_store.py` as the sole writer of analytic/assessment and new cold episode tables, and `postgres_finding_store.py` as the sole Finding writer, with a caller-owned transaction spanning both and job completion. The current Finding store hardcodes dispatch mechanism/title/matcher and independently opens transactions: factor domain-specific values and transaction-capable methods rather than calling the dispatch writer for cold. Migration `0022` must widen the existing `finding_revision.transition` CHECK for `BASIS_REFRESHED` and `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` if those labels are used; retain `SUPPORT_CHANGED`. `cold_episode.id` is a UUID derived deterministically from the stated SHA-256 episode-key tuple (UUIDv5 under a fixed cold namespace), not the raw SHA text; `anchor_time` is the first qualifying accepted sample/request source time. Add nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id` self-FK to `cold_episode` for the promised split relation, with cycle/self checks. The first crossing is anchored by its **accepted evidence UUID**, while a request uses its accepted event UUID; a sample has no event ID. A late record changes revisions and bounds, not episode identity, unless the anchor is invalidated. A `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` FindingRevision retains the old Finding as history; it never deletes it. `finding_revision` has NOT NULL analytic/run/assessment/basis fields: every cold FindingRevision must cite the selected cold analytic run and assessment revision and compute the existing four SHA fields from exact accepted IDs, including canonical `[]` for empty support. The current Finding read/index filters and lineage must be widened by domain/analytic; keep mini-grid filters unchanged. Rule 8 must add all three cold episode tables to `$assessTables` **before** `$assessInserts` is derived, and only the assessment adapter may insert them; test foreign-writer and mutation refusals. This is W2-03 work, not an implicit consequence-writer task.
 
 Role set `cold-roles-1` is an immutable named version in `sites/binding_roles.py`; register in `ROLE_SET_BY_SITE_TYPE` and `SERVED_ROLE_SETS_BY_SITE_TYPE` (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_roles.py:17-27,117-130`). The values below are canonical. `ONE` means one binding per Site, `PER_ASSET` one per named asset. All source/device/signal rows must be registered and linked to the same Site and effective Foundation revision. No binding may silently cover two incompatible roles. Numeric normalization is IDENTITY in v1; state ENUM needs an explicit finite mapping; Celsius conversion is not implicit.

The W2-00 closed v4 table and W2-01 snapshot rows must name these exact roles; no channel may be bound twice. On cold panels replace status labels `Mapped`/`Not mapped` with `Configured`/`Needs a source`; use `Reporting`/`No report received` only when an actual selected evidence/contact read proves it. Keep panel titles fixed by the rendered-panel manifest.
+
+**W2-02 code seam closure.** Add cold model and publication identities to `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` `MODEL_PROFILES`/`PUBLICATION_PROFILES`, `host/execution_adapter.py` `EXECUTABLE_MODELS`, `host/lab_execution.py` model/reporting selection and its `_LIFECYCLE` validator, and `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py` `_MAPPING_BY_PUBLICATION_PROFILE`. The latter currently falls back to fuel mapping for an unknown profile: explicitly refuse an unknown cold/EV profile instead of falling back. Add the cold scenario state keys/scopes to the closed state-reference and execution registries and a separate cold pack/observation transform; `kernel/execute.py` currently assumes mini-grid/fuel model laws and does not acquire a new pack merely by receiving a `ModelSpec`. Use a typed pack dispatch boundary or extend generic laws with proof that existing mini-grid vectors are unchanged. `host/lab_execution.py` currently refuses non-measurement staging as V3; select the v4 stage/Batch V2 path by the frozen cold profile, including stable event IDs and pinned registered bindings, while preserving the V3 path. `profiles.py` owns publication cadence; Foundation declares availability/unit only. Freeze observation/profile/pack identity before Draft and include them in run identity. W2-02 may not call S2 with a synthetic unregistered mapping or use the W2-00 test-only injected basis.
+
+**W2-03 code seam closure.** The current `assessment_worker.py` is dispatch-only and `postgres_assessment_store.py` selects numeric readings; add a cold-specific typed evidence query/worker and adapter methods that select both v4 measurement and EVENT rows by accepted disposition decision time, tenant/Site, role, selected Foundation/binding and half-open source interval. Do not coerce event null values to readings. Reuse the existing `recompute_job` table under `domain='cold_chain'`, but give it a separate domain-specific lease and worker path; existing dispatch selection/job behavior remains unchanged. Extend `postgres_assessment_store.py` as the sole writer of analytic/assessment and new cold episode tables, and `postgres_finding_store.py` as the sole Finding writer, with a caller-owned transaction spanning both and job completion. The current Finding store hardcodes dispatch mechanism/title/matcher and independently opens transactions: factor domain-specific values and transaction-capable methods rather than calling the dispatch writer for cold. Migration `0022` must widen the existing `finding_revision.transition` CHECK for `BASIS_REFRESHED` and `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` if those labels are used; retain `SUPPORT_CHANGED`. `cold_episode.id` is a UUID derived deterministically from the stated SHA-256 episode-key tuple (UUIDv5 under a fixed cold namespace), not the raw SHA text; `anchor_time` is the first qualifying accepted sample/request source time. Add nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id` self-FK to `cold_episode` for the promised split relation, with cycle/self checks. The first crossing is anchored by its **accepted evidence UUID**, while a request uses its accepted event UUID; a sample has no event ID. A late record changes revisions and bounds, not episode identity, unless the anchor is invalidated. A `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` FindingRevision retains the old Finding as history; it never deletes it. `finding_revision` has NOT NULL analytic/run/assessment/basis fields: every cold FindingRevision must cite the selected cold analytic run and assessment revision and compute the existing four SHA fields from exact accepted IDs, including canonical `[]` for empty support. The current Finding read/index filters and lineage must be widened by domain/analytic; keep mini-grid filters unchanged. Rule 8 must add all three cold episode tables to `$assessTables` **before** `$assessInserts` is derived, and only the assessment adapter may insert them; test foreign-writer and mutation refusals. This is W2-03 work, not an implicit consequence-writer task.
 
 Role set `cold-roles-1` is an immutable named version in `sites/binding_roles.py`; register in `ROLE_SET_BY_SITE_TYPE` and `SERVED_ROLE_SETS_BY_SITE_TYPE` (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_roles.py:17-27,117-130`). The values below are canonical. `ONE` means one binding per Site, `PER_ASSET` one per named asset. All source/device/signal rows must be registered and linked to the same Site and effective Foundation revision. No binding may silently cover two incompatible roles. Numeric normalization is IDENTITY in v1; state ENUM needs an explicit finite mapping; Celsius conversion is not implicit.

-## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `GRID/METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. Exactly one such incoming room link per configured primary chamber probe and at most one primary probe per room; complete readiness requires exactly one per room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` or no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. This is contact, not equipment health. `bindings[].cadence` is the declared cadence of the frozen cold publication profile, `300` seconds for this profile, exposed as `cadence_seconds`; it is neither inferred from readings nor stored on a binding. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.
+
+**W2-02 channel closure.** Add `grid_power_event` / METER, EVENT `POWER_LOSS|POWER_RESTORED`, ONE, `event` unit. Replace the two impossible `backup_request` and `backup_result` channel bindings with **one** required `backup_start_event` / GENERATOR, EVENT `START_REQUEST|START_RESULT`, ONE; require both kinds in capability/readiness, while one registered `(generator-controller,start-event)` channel carries both. `grid_state` remains its separate `(grid-meter,state)` channel. `transfer_result`, `door_event` and `manual_temperature` keep separate channels. Migration `0022` must widen the existing `finding_revision.transition` CHECK for `BASIS_REFRESHED` and `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` if those labels are used; retain `SUPPORT_CHANGED`. `cold_episode.id` is a UUID derived deterministically from the stated SHA-256 episode-key tuple (UUIDv5 under a fixed cold namespace), not the raw SHA text; `anchor_time` is the first qualifying accepted sample/request source time. Add nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id` self-FK to `cold_episode` for the promised split relation, with cycle/self checks. The first crossing is anchored by its **accepted evidence UUID**, while a request uses its accepted event UUID; a sample has no event ID. A late record changes revisions and bounds, not episode identity, unless the anchor is invalidated. A `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` FindingRevision retains the old Finding as history; it never deletes it. `finding_revision` has NOT NULL analytic/run/assessment/basis fields: every cold FindingRevision must cite the selected cold analytic run and assessment revision and compute the existing four SHA fields from exact accepted IDs, including canonical `[]` for empty support. The current Finding read/index filters and lineage must be widened by domain/analytic; keep mini-grid filters unchanged. Rule 8 must add all three cold episode tables to `$assessTables` **before** `$assessInserts` is derived, and only the assessment adapter may insert them; test foreign-writer and mutation refusals. This is W2-03 work, not an implicit consequence-writer task.
 
 Role set `cold-roles-1` is an immutable named version in `sites/binding_roles.py`; register in `ROLE_SET_BY_SITE_TYPE` and `SERVED_ROLE_SETS_BY_SITE_TYPE` (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_roles.py:17-27,117-130`). The values below are canonical. `ONE` means one binding per Site, `PER_ASSET` one per named asset. All source/device/signal rows must be registered and linked to the same Site and effective Foundation revision. No binding may silently cover two incompatible roles. Numeric normalization is IDENTITY in v1; state ENUM needs an explicit finite mapping; Celsius conversion is not implicit.

-## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `GRID/METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. Exactly one such incoming room link per configured primary chamber probe and at most one primary probe per room; complete readiness requires exactly one per room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` or no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. This is contact, not equipment health. `bindings[].cadence` is the declared cadence of the frozen cold publication profile, `300` seconds for this profile, exposed as `cadence_seconds`; it is neither inferred from readings nor stored on a binding. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.
+
+**W2-02 channel closure.** Add `grid_power_event` / METER, EVENT `POWER_LOSS|POWER_RESTORED`, ONE, `event` unit. Replace the two impossible `backup_request` and `backup_result` channel bindings with **one** required `backup_start_event` / GENERATOR, EVENT `START_REQUEST|START_RESULT`, ONE; require both kinds in capability/readiness, while one registered `(generator-controller,start-event)` channel carries both. `grid_state` remains its separate `(grid-meter,state)` channel. `transfer_result`, `door_event` and `manual_temperature` keep separate channels. The W2-00 closed v4 table and W2-01 snapshot rows must name these exact roles; no channel may be bound twice. On cold panels replace status labels `Mapped`/`Not mapped` with `Configured`/`Needs a source`; use `Reporting`/`No report received` only when an actual selected evidence/contact read proves it. Keep panel titles fixed by the rendered-panel manifest.
+
+**W2-02 code seam closure.** Add cold model and publication identities to `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` `MODEL_PROFILES`/`PUBLICATION_PROFILES`, `host/execution_adapter.py` `EXECUTABLE_MODELS`, `host/lab_execution.py` model/reporting selection and its `_LIFECYCLE` validator, and `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py` `_MAPPING_BY_PUBLICATION_PROFILE`. The latter currently falls back to fuel mapping for an unknown profile: explicitly refuse an unknown cold/EV profile instead of falling back. Add the cold scenario state keys/scopes to the closed state-reference and execution registries and a separate cold pack/observation transform; `kernel/execute.py` currently assumes mini-grid/fuel model laws and does not acquire a new pack merely by receiving a `ModelSpec`. Use a typed pack dispatch boundary or extend generic laws with proof that existing mini-grid vectors are unchanged. `host/lab_execution.py` currently refuses non-measurement staging as V3; select the v4 stage/Batch V2 path by the frozen cold profile, including stable event IDs and pinned registered bindings, while preserving the V3 path. `profiles.py` owns publication cadence; Foundation declares availability/unit only. Freeze observation/profile/pack identity before Draft and include them in run identity. W2-02 may not call S2 with a synthetic unregistered mapping or use the W2-00 test-only injected basis.
+
+**W2-03 code seam closure.** The current `assessment_worker.py` is dispatch-only and `postgres_assessment_store.py` selects numeric readings; add a cold-specific typed evidence query/worker and adapter methods that select both v4 measurement and EVENT rows by accepted disposition decision time, tenant/Site, role, selected Foundation/binding and half-open source interval. Do not coerce event null values to readings. Reuse the existing `recompute_job` table under `domain='cold_chain'`, but give it a separate domain-specific lease and worker path; existing dispatch selection/job behavior remains unchanged. Extend `postgres_assessment_store.py` as the sole writer of analytic/assessment and new cold episode tables, and `postgres_finding_store.py` as the sole Finding writer, with a caller-owned transaction spanning both and job completion. The current Finding store hardcodes dispatch mechanism/title/matcher and independently opens transactions: factor domain-specific values and transaction-capable methods rather than calling the dispatch writer for cold. The current Finding store hardcodes dispatch mechanism/title/matcher and independently opens transactions: factor domain-specific values and transaction-capable methods rather than calling the dispatch writer for cold. Migration `0022` must widen the existing `finding_revision.transition` CHECK for `BASIS_REFRESHED` and `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` if those labels are used; retain `SUPPORT_CHANGED`. `cold_episode.id` is a UUID derived deterministically from the stated SHA-256 episode-key tuple (UUIDv5 under a fixed cold namespace), not the raw SHA text; `anchor_time` is the first qualifying accepted sample/request source time. Add nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id` self-FK to `cold_episode` for the promised split relation, with cycle/self checks. The first crossing is anchored by its **accepted evidence UUID**, while a request uses its accepted event UUID; a sample has no event ID. A late record changes revisions and bounds, not episode identity, unless the anchor is invalidated. A `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` FindingRevision retains the old Finding as history; it never deletes it. `finding_revision` has NOT NULL analytic/run/assessment/basis fields: every cold FindingRevision must cite the selected cold analytic run and assessment revision and compute the existing four SHA fields from exact accepted IDs, including canonical `[]` for empty support. The current Finding read/index filters and lineage must be widened by domain/analytic; keep mini-grid filters unchanged. Rule 8 must add all three cold episode tables to `$assessTables` **before** `$assessInserts` is derived, and only the assessment adapter may insert them; test foreign-writer and mutation refusals. This is W2-03 work, not an implicit consequence-writer task.
 
 Role set `cold-roles-1` is an immutable named version in `sites/binding_roles.py`; register in `ROLE_SET_BY_SITE_TYPE` and `SERVED_ROLE_SETS_BY_SITE_TYPE` (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_roles.py:17-27,117-130`). The values below are canonical. `ONE` means one binding per Site, `PER_ASSET` one per named asset. All source/device/signal rows must be registered and linked to the same Site and effective Foundation revision. No binding may silently cover two incompatible roles. Numeric normalization is IDENTITY in v1; state ENUM needs an explicit finite mapping; Celsius conversion is not implicit.

-## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` or no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. This is contact, not equipment health. `bindings[].cadence` is the declared cadence of the frozen cold publication profile, `300` seconds for this profile, exposed as `cadence_seconds`; it is neither inferred from readings nor stored on a binding. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.
+
+**W2-02 channel closure.** Add `grid_power_event` / METER, EVENT `POWER_LOSS|POWER_RESTORED`, ONE, `event` unit. Replace the two impossible `backup_request` and `backup_result` channel bindings with **one** required `backup_start_event` / GENERATOR, EVENT `START_REQUEST|START_RESULT`, ONE; require both kinds in capability/readiness, while one registered `(generator-controller,start-event)` channel carries both. `grid_state` remains its separate `(grid-meter,state)` channel. `transfer_result`, `door_event` and `manual_temperature` keep separate channels. The W2-00 closed v4 table and W2-01 snapshot rows must name these exact roles; no channel may be bound twice. On cold panels replace status labels `Mapped`/`Not mapped` with `Configured`/`Needs a source`; use `Reporting`/`No report received` only when an actual selected evidence/contact read proves it. Keep panel titles fixed by the rendered-panel manifest.
+
+**W2-02 code seam closure.** Add cold model and publication identities to `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` `MODEL_PROFILES`/`PUBLICATION_PROFILES`, `host/execution_adapter.py` `EXECUTABLE_MODELS`, `host/lab_execution.py` model/reporting selection and its `_LIFECYCLE` validator, and `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py` `_MAPPING_BY_PUBLICATION_PROFILE`. The latter currently falls back to fuel mapping for an unknown profile: explicitly refuse an unknown cold/EV profile instead of falling back. Add the cold scenario state keys/scopes to the closed state-reference and execution registries and a separate cold pack/observation transform; `kernel/execute.py` currently assumes mini-grid/fuel model laws and does not acquire a new pack merely by receiving a `ModelSpec`. Use a typed pack dispatch boundary or extend generic laws with proof that existing mini-grid vectors are unchanged. `host/lab_execution.py` currently refuses non-measurement staging as V3; select the v4 stage/Batch V2 path by the frozen cold profile, including stable event IDs and pinned registered bindings, while preserving the V3 path. `profiles.py` owns publication cadence; Foundation declares availability/unit only. Freeze observation/profile/pack identity before Draft and include them in run identity. W2-02 may not call S2 with a synthetic unregistered mapping or use the W2-00 test-only injected basis.
+
+**W2-03 code seam closure.** The current `assessment_worker.py` is dispatch-only and `postgres_assessment_store.py` selects numeric readings; add a cold-specific typed evidence query/worker and adapter methods that select both v4 measurement and EVENT rows by accepted disposition decision time, tenant/Site, role, selected Foundation/binding and half-open source interval. Do not coerce event null values to readings. -## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` or no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. This is contact, not equipment health. `bindings[].cadence` is the declared cadence of the frozen cold publication profile, `300` seconds for this profile, exposed as `cadence_seconds`; it is neither inferred from readings nor stored on a binding. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.
+
+**W2-02 channel closure.** Add `grid_power_event` / METER, EVENT `POWER_LOSS|POWER_RESTORED`, ONE, `event` unit. Replace the two impossible `backup_request` and `backup_result` channel bindings with **one** required `backup_start_event` / GENERATOR, EVENT `START_REQUEST|START_RESULT`, ONE; require both kinds in capability/readiness, while one registered `(generator-controller,start-event)` channel carries both. `grid_state` remains its separate `(grid-meter,state)` channel. `transfer_result`, `door_event` and `manual_temperature` keep separate channels. The W2-00 closed v4 table and W2-01 snapshot rows must name these exact roles; no channel may be bound twice. On cold panels replace status labels `Mapped`/`Not mapped` with `Configured`/`Needs a source`; use `Reporting`/`No report received` only when an actual selected evidence/contact read proves it. Keep panel titles fixed by the rendered-panel manifest.
+
+**W2-02 code seam closure.** Add cold model and publication identities to `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` `MODEL_PROFILES`/`PUBLICATION_PROFILES`, `host/execution_adapter.py` `EXECUTABLE_MODELS`, `host/lab_execution.py` model/reporting selection and its `_LIFECYCLE` validator, and `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py` `_MAPPING_BY_PUBLICATION_PROFILE`. The latter currently falls back to fuel mapping for an unknown profile: explicitly refuse an unknown cold/EV profile instead of falling back. Add the cold scenario state keys/scopes to the closed state-reference and execution registries and a separate cold pack/observation transform; `kernel/execute.py` currently assumes mini-grid/fuel model laws and does not acquire a new pack merely by receiving a `ModelSpec`. Use a typed pack dispatch boundary or extend generic laws with proof that existing mini-grid vectors are unchanged. `host/lab_execution.py` currently refuses non-measurement staging as V3; select the v4 stage/Batch V2 path by the frozen cold profile, including stable event IDs and pinned registered bindings, while preserving the V3 path. `profiles.py` owns publication cadence; Foundation declares availability/unit only. Freeze observation/profile/pack identity before Draft and include them in run identity. W2-02 may not call S2 with a synthetic unregistered mapping or use the W2-00 test-only injected basis.

The current Finding store hardcodes dispatch mechanism/title/matcher and independently opens transactions: factor domain-specific values and transaction-capable methods rather than calling the dispatch writer for cold. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` or no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. This is contact, not equipment health. `bindings[].cadence` is the declared cadence of the frozen cold publication profile, `300` seconds for this profile, exposed as `cadence_seconds`; it is neither inferred from readings nor stored on a binding. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.
+
+**W2-02 channel closure.** Add `grid_power_event` / METER, EVENT `POWER_LOSS|POWER_RESTORED`, ONE, `event` unit. Replace the two impossible `backup_request` and `backup_result` channel bindings with **one** required `backup_start_event` / GENERATOR, EVENT `START_REQUEST|START_RESULT`, ONE; require both kinds in capability/readiness, while one registered `(generator-controller,start-event)` channel carries both. `grid_state` remains its separate `(grid-meter,state)` channel. `transfer_result`, `door_event` and `manual_temperature` keep separate channels. The W2-00 closed v4 table and W2-01 snapshot rows must name these exact roles; no channel may be bound twice. On cold panels replace status labels `Mapped`/`Not mapped` with `Configured`/`Needs a source`; use `Reporting`/`No report received` only when an actual selected evidence/contact read proves it. Keep panel titles fixed by the rendered-panel manifest.
+
+**W2-02 code seam closure.** Add cold model and publication identities to `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` `MODEL_PROFILES`/`PUBLICATION_PROFILES`, `host/execution_adapter.py` `EXECUTABLE_MODELS`, `host/lab_execution.py` model/reporting selection and its `_LIFECYCLE` validator, and `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py` `_MAPPING_BY_PUBLICATION_PROFILE`. The latter currently falls back to fuel mapping for an unknown profile: explicitly refuse an unknown cold/EV profile instead of falling back. Add the cold scenario state keys/scopes to the closed state-reference and execution registries and a separate cold pack/observation transform; `kernel/execute.py` currently assumes mini-grid/fuel model laws and does not acquire a new pack merely by receiving a `ModelSpec`. Use a typed pack dispatch boundary or extend generic laws with proof that existing mini-grid vectors are unchanged. `host/lab_execution.py` currently refuses non-measurement staging as V3; select the v4 stage/Batch V2 path by the frozen cold profile, including stable event IDs and pinned registered bindings, while preserving the V3 path. `profiles.py` owns publication cadence; Foundation declares availability/unit only. Freeze observation/profile/pack identity before Draft and include them in run identity. W2-02 may not call S2 with a synthetic unregistered mapping or use the W2-00 test-only injected basis.
+
+**W2-03 code seam closure.** The current `assessment_worker.py` is dispatch-only and `postgres_assessment_store.py` selects numeric readings; add a cold-specific typed evidence query/worker and adapter methods that select both v4 measurement and EVENT rows by accepted disposition decision time, tenant/Site, role, selected Foundation/binding and half-open source interval. Do not coerce event null values to readings. Reuse the existing `recompute_job` table under `domain='cold_chain'`, but give it a separate domain-specific lease and worker path; existing dispatch selection/job behavior remains unchanged. Extend `postgres_assessment_store.py` as the sole writer of analytic/assessment and new cold episode tables, and `postgres_finding_store.py` as the sole Finding writer, with a caller-owned transaction spanning both and job completion. The current Finding store hardcodes dispatch mechanism/title/matcher and independently opens transactions: factor domain-specific values and transaction-capable methods rather than calling the dispatch writer for cold. Migration `0022` must widen the existing `finding_revision.transition` CHECK for `BASIS_REFRESHED` and `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` if those labels are used; retain `SUPPORT_CHANGED`. `cold_episode.id` is a UUID derived deterministically from the stated SHA-256 episode-key tuple (UUIDv5 under a fixed cold namespace), not the raw SHA text; `anchor_time` is the first qualifying accepted sample/request source time. Add nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id` self-FK to `cold_episode` for the promised split relation, with cycle/self checks. The first crossing is anchored by its **accepted evidence UUID**, while a request uses its accepted event UUID; a sample has no event ID. A late record changes revisions and bounds, not episode identity, unless the anchor is invalidated. A `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` FindingRevision retains the old Finding as history; it never deletes it. `finding_revision` has NOT NULL analytic/run/assessment/basis fields: every cold FindingRevision must cite the selected cold analytic run and assessment revision and compute the existing four SHA fields from exact accepted IDs, including canonical `[]` for empty support. The current Finding read/index filters and lineage must be widened by domain/analytic; keep mini-grid filters unchanged. Rule 8 must add all three cold episode tables to `$assessTables` **before** `$assessInserts` is derived, and only the assessment adapter may insert them; test foreign-writer and mutation refusals. This is W2-03 work, not an implicit consequence-writer task.

The values below are canonical. `ONE` means one binding per Site, `PER_ASSET` one per named asset. All source/device/signal rows must be registered and linked to the same Site and effective Foundation revision. No binding may silently cover two incompatible roles. Numeric normalization is IDENTITY in v1; state ENUM needs an explicit finite mapping; Celsius conversion is not implicit.

+## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
