-## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` or no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. This is contact, not equipment health. `bindings[].cadence` is the declared cadence of the frozen cold publication profile, `300` seconds for this profile, exposed as `cadence_seconds`; it is neither inferred from readings nor stored on a binding. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.
+
+**W2-02 channel closure.** Add `grid_power_event` / METER, EVENT `POWER_LOSS|POWER_RESTORED`, ONE, `event` unit. Replace the two impossible `backup_request` and `backup_result` channel bindings with **one** required `backup_start_event` / GENERATOR, EVENT `START_REQUEST|START_RESULT`, ONE; require both kinds in capability/readiness, while one registered `(generator-controller,start-event)` channel carries both. `grid_state` remains its separate `(grid-meter,state)` channel. `transfer_result`, `door_event` and `manual_temperature` keep separate channels. The W2-00 closed v4 table and W2-01 snapshot rows must name these exact roles; no channel may be bound twice. On cold panels replace status labels `Mapped`/`Not mapped` with `Configured`/`Needs a source`; use `Reporting`/`No report received` only when an actual selected evidence/contact read proves it. Keep panel titles fixed by the rendered-panel manifest.
+
+**W2-02 code seam closure.** Add cold model and publication identities to `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` `MODEL_PROFILES`/`PUBLICATION_PROFILES`, `host/execution_adapter.py` `EXECUTABLE_MODELS`, `host/lab_execution.py` model/reporting selection and its `_LIFECYCLE` validator, and `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py` `_MAPPING_BY_PUBLICATION_PROFILE`. The latter currently falls back to fuel mapping for an unknown profile: explicitly refuse an unknown cold/EV profile instead of falling back. Add the cold scenario state keys/scopes to the closed state-reference and execution registries and a separate cold pack/observation transform; `kernel/execute.py` currently assumes mini-grid/fuel model laws and does not acquire a new pack merely by receiving a `ModelSpec`. Use a typed pack dispatch boundary or extend generic laws with proof that existing mini-grid vectors are unchanged. `host/lab_execution.py` currently refuses non-measurement staging as V3; select the v4 stage/Batch V2 path by the frozen cold profile, including stable event IDs and pinned registered bindings, while preserving the V3 path. `profiles.py` owns publication cadence; Foundation declares availability/unit only. Freeze observation/profile/pack identity before Draft and include them in run identity. W2-02 may not call S2 with a synthetic unregistered mapping or use the W2-00 test-only injected basis.
+
+**W2-03 code seam closure.** The current `assessment_worker.py` is dispatch-only and `postgres_assessment_store.py` selects numeric readings; add a cold-specific typed evidence query/worker and adapter methods that select both v4 measurement and EVENT rows by accepted disposition decision time, tenant/Site, role, selected Foundation/binding and half-open source interval. Do not coerce event null values to readings. Reuse the existing `recompute_job` table under `domain='cold_chain'`, but give it a separate domain-specific lease and worker path; existing dispatch selection/job behavior remains unchanged. Extend `postgres_assessment_store.py` as the sole writer of analytic/assessment and new cold episode tables, and `postgres_finding_store.py` as the sole Finding writer, with a caller-owned transaction spanning both and job completion. The current Finding store hardcodes dispatch mechanism/title/matcher and independently opens transactions: factor domain-specific values and transaction-capable methods rather than calling the dispatch writer for cold. Migration `0022` must widen the existing `finding_revision.transition` CHECK for `BASIS_REFRESHED` and `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` if those labels are used; retain `SUPPORT_CHANGED`. `cold_episode.id` is a UUID derived deterministically from the stated SHA-256 episode-key tuple (UUIDv5 under a fixed cold namespace), not the raw SHA text; `anchor_time` is the first qualifying accepted sample/request source time. Add nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id` self-FK to `cold_episode` for the promised split relation, with cycle/self checks. The first crossing is anchored by its **accepted evidence UUID**, while a request uses its accepted event UUID; a sample has no event ID. A late record changes revisions and bounds, not episode identity, unless the anchor is invalidated. A `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` FindingRevision retains the old Finding as history; it never deletes it. `finding_revision` has NOT NULL analytic/run/assessment/basis fields: every cold FindingRevision must cite the selected cold analytic run and assessment revision and compute the existing four SHA fields from exact accepted IDs, including canonical `[]` for empty support. The current Finding read/index filters and lineage must be widened by domain/analytic; keep mini-grid filters unchanged. Rule 8 must add all three cold episode tables to `$assessTables` **before** `$assessInserts` is derived, and only the assessment adapter may insert them; test foreign-writer and mutation refusals. This is W2-03 work, not an implicit consequence-writer task.
 
 Role set `cold-roles-1` is an immutable named version in `sites/binding_roles.py`; register in `ROLE_SET_BY_SITE_TYPE` and `SERVED_ROLE_SETS_BY_SITE_TYPE` (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_roles.py:17-27,117-130`). The values below are canonical. `ONE` means one binding per Site, `PER_ASSET` one per named asset. All source/device/signal rows must be registered and linked to the same Site and effective Foundation revision. No binding may silently cover two incompatible roles. Numeric normalization is IDENTITY in v1; state ENUM needs an explicit finite mapping; Celsius conversion is not implicit.

CREATE TABLE finding_revision (
    id uuid PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id uuid NOT NULL,
    site_id uuid NOT NULL,
    finding_id uuid NOT NULL,
    revision_no integer NOT NULL CHECK (revision_no >= 1),
    transition text NOT NULL CHECK (transition IN ('OPENED', 'SUPPORT_CHANGED', 'MODEL_REEVALUATED', 'SPLIT',
        'MERGED', 'MERGED_INTO', 'UNCONFIRMED', 'NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED', 'BASIS_REFRESHED', 'REOPENED')),
    lifecycle text NOT NULL CHECK (lifecycle IN ('SUPPORTED', 'UNCONFIRMED', 'NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED', 'MERGED')),
    claim_level text NOT NULL,
    title text NOT NULL,
    mechanism text NOT NULL,
    matcher_version text NOT NULL,
    analytic_id text NOT NULL,
    model_version text NOT NULL,
    assessment_revision_id uuid NOT NULL,
    analytic_run_id uuid NOT NULL,
    window_assessment_revision_id uuid NULL,
    episode_start timestamptz NOT NULL,
    episode_end timestamptz NOT NULL,
    candidate_minutes numeric(12, 4) NOT NULL,
    necessary_minutes numeric(12, 4) NOT NULL,
    indeterminate_minutes numeric(12, 4) NOT NULL,
    claim jsonb NOT NULL,
    limitations jsonb NOT NULL,
    basis_sha256 text NOT NULL,
    support_sha256 text NOT NULL,
    cited_evidence_sha256 text NOT NULL,
    input_evidence_sha256 text NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT finding_revision_finding_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, finding_id)
        REFERENCES finding(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT finding_revision_assessment_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, assessment_revision_id)
        REFERENCES assessment_revision(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT finding_revision_run_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, analytic_run_id)
        REFERENCES analytic_run(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT finding_revision_window_fk FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id, site_id, window_assessment_revision_id)
        REFERENCES assessment_revision(tenant_id, site_id, id),
    CONSTRAINT finding_revision_bounds CHECK (episode_start <= episode_end),
    CONSTRAINT finding_revision_no_unique UNIQUE (finding_id, revision_no),
    CONSTRAINT finding_revision_basis_unique UNIQUE (finding_id, basis_sha256, transition),
    CONSTRAINT finding_revision_site_unique UNIQUE (tenant_id, site_id, id)
);

-## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` or no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. This is contact, not equipment health. `bindings[].cadence` is the declared cadence of the frozen cold publication profile, `300` seconds for this profile, exposed as `cadence_seconds`; it is neither inferred from readings nor stored on a binding. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.

-## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` or no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. This is contact, not equipment health. `bindings[].cadence` is the declared cadence of the frozen cold publication profile, `300` seconds for this profile, exposed as `cadence_seconds`; it is neither inferred from readings nor stored on a binding. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.
+
+**W2-02 channel closure.** Add `grid_power_event` / METER, EVENT `POWER_LOSS|POWER_RESTORED`, ONE, `event` unit. Replace the two impossible `backup_request` and `backup_result` channel bindings with **one** required `backup_start_event` / GENERATOR, EVENT `START_REQUEST|START_RESULT`, ONE; require both kinds in capability/readiness, while one registered `(generator-controller,start-event)` channel carries both. `grid_state` remains its separate `(grid-meter,state)` channel. `transfer_result`, `door_event` and `manual_temperature` keep separate channels. The W2-00 closed v4 table and W2-01 snapshot rows must name these exact roles; no channel may be bound twice. On cold panels replace status labels `Mapped`/`Not mapped` with `Configured`/`Needs a source`; use `Reporting`/`No report received` only when an actual selected evidence/contact read proves it. Keep panel titles fixed by the rendered-panel manifest.
+
+**W2-02 code seam closure.** Add cold model and publication identities to `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` `MODEL_PROFILES`/`PUBLICATION_PROFILES`, `host/execution_adapter.py` `EXECUTABLE_MODELS`, `host/lab_execution.py` model/reporting selection and its `_LIFECYCLE` validator, and `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py` `_MAPPING_BY_PUBLICATION_PROFILE`. The latter currently falls back to fuel mapping for an unknown profile: explicitly refuse an unknown cold/EV profile instead of falling back. Add the cold scenario state keys/scopes to the closed state-reference and execution registries and a separate cold pack/observation transform; `kernel/execute.py` currently assumes mini-grid/fuel model laws and does not acquire a new pack merely by receiving a `ModelSpec`. Use a typed pack dispatch boundary or extend generic laws with proof that existing mini-grid vectors are unchanged. `host/lab_execution.py` currently refuses non-measurement staging as V3; select the v4 stage/Batch V2 path by the frozen cold profile, including stable event IDs and pinned registered bindings, while preserving the V3 path. `profiles.py` owns publication cadence; Foundation declares availability/unit only. Freeze observation/profile/pack identity before Draft and include them in run identity. W2-02 may not call S2 with a synthetic unregistered mapping or use the W2-00 test-only injected basis.
+
+**W2-03 code seam closure.** The current `assessment_worker.py` is dispatch-only and `postgres_assessment_store.py` selects numeric readings; add a cold-specific typed evidence query/worker and adapter methods that select both v4 measurement and EVENT rows by accepted disposition decision time, tenant/Site, role, selected Foundation/binding and half-open source interval. Do not coerce event null values to readings. Reuse the existing `recompute_job` table under `domain='cold_chain'`, but give it a separate domain-specific lease and worker path; existing dispatch selection/job behavior remains unchanged. Extend `postgres_assessment_store.py` as the sole writer of analytic/assessment and new cold episode tables, and `postgres_finding_store.py` as the sole Finding writer, with a caller-owned transaction spanning both and job completion. The current Finding store hardcodes dispatch mechanism/title/matcher and independently opens transactions: factor domain-specific values and transaction-capable methods rather than calling the dispatch writer for cold. Migration `0022` must widen the existing `finding_revision.transition` CHECK for `BASIS_REFRESHED` and `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` if those labels are used; retain `SUPPORT_CHANGED`. `cold_episode.id` is a UUID derived deterministically from the stated SHA-256 episode-key tuple (UUIDv5 under a fixed cold namespace), not the raw SHA text; `anchor_time` is the first qualifying accepted sample/request source time. Add nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id` self-FK to `cold_episode` for the promised split relation, with cycle/self checks. The first crossing is anchored by its **accepted evidence UUID**, while a request uses its accepted event UUID; a sample has no event ID. A late record changes revisions and bounds, not episode identity, unless the anchor is invalidated. A `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` FindingRevision retains the old Finding as history; it never deletes it. `finding_revision` has NOT NULL analytic/run/assessment/basis fields: every cold FindingRevision must cite the selected cold analytic run and assessment revision and compute the existing four SHA fields from exact accepted IDs, including canonical `[]` for empty support. The current Finding read/index filters and lineage must be widened by domain/analytic; keep mini-grid filters unchanged. Rule 8 must add all three cold episode tables to `$assessTables` **before** `$assessInserts` is derived, and only the assessment adapter may insert them; test foreign-writer and mutation refusals. This is W2-03 work, not an implicit consequence-writer task.
 
 Role set `cold-roles-1` is an immutable named version in `sites/binding_roles.py`; register in `ROLE_SET_BY_SITE_TYPE` and `SERVED_ROLE_SETS_BY_SITE_TYPE` (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_roles.py:17-27,117-130`). The values below are canonical. `ONE` means one binding per Site, `PER_ASSET` one per named asset. All source/device/signal rows must be registered and linked to the same Site and effective Foundation revision. No binding may silently cover two incompatible roles. Numeric normalization is IDENTITY in v1; state ENUM needs an explicit finite mapping; Celsius conversion is not implicit.

-## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` or no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. This is contact, not equipment health. `bindings[].cadence` is the declared cadence of the frozen cold publication profile, `300` seconds for this profile, exposed as `cadence_seconds`; it is neither inferred from readings nor stored on a binding. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.
+
+**W2-02 channel closure.** Add `grid_power_event` / METER, EVENT `POWER_LOSS|POWER_RESTORED`, ONE, `event` unit. Replace the two impossible `backup_request` and `backup_result` channel bindings with **one** required `backup_start_event` / GENERATOR, EVENT `START_REQUEST|START_RESULT`, ONE; require both kinds in capability/readiness, while one registered `(generator-controller,start-event)` channel carries both. `grid_state` remains its separate `(grid-meter,state)` channel. `transfer_result`, `door_event` and `manual_temperature` keep separate channels. The W2-00 closed v4 table and W2-01 snapshot rows must name these exact roles; no channel may be bound twice. On cold panels replace status labels `Mapped`/`Not mapped` with `Configured`/`Needs a source`; use `Reporting`/`No report received` only when an actual selected evidence/contact read proves it. Keep panel titles fixed by the rendered-panel manifest.
+
+**W2-02 code seam closure.** Add cold model and publication identities to `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` `MODEL_PROFILES`/`PUBLICATION_PROFILES`, `host/execution_adapter.py` `EXECUTABLE_MODELS`, `host/lab_execution.py` model/reporting selection and its `_LIFECYCLE` validator, and `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py` `_MAPPING_BY_PUBLICATION_PROFILE`. The latter currently falls back to fuel mapping for an unknown profile: explicitly refuse an unknown cold/EV profile instead of falling back. Add the cold scenario state keys/scopes to the closed state-reference and execution registries and a separate cold pack/observation transform; `kernel/execute.py` currently assumes mini-grid/fuel model laws and does not acquire a new pack merely by receiving a `ModelSpec`. Use a typed pack dispatch boundary or extend generic laws with proof that existing mini-grid vectors are unchanged. `host/lab_execution.py` currently refuses non-measurement staging as V3; select the v4 stage/Batch V2 path by the frozen cold profile, including stable event IDs and pinned registered bindings, while preserving the V3 path. `profiles.py` owns publication cadence; Foundation declares availability/unit only. Freeze observation/profile/pack identity before Draft and include them in run identity. W2-02 may not call S2 with a synthetic unregistered mapping or use the W2-00 test-only injected basis.
+
+**W2-03 code seam closure.** The current `assessment_worker.py` is dispatch-only and `postgres_assessment_store.py` selects numeric readings; add a cold-specific typed evidence query/worker and adapter methods that select both v4 measurement and EVENT rows by accepted disposition decision time, tenant/Site, role, selected Foundation/binding and half-open source interval. Do not coerce event null values to readings. Reuse the existing `recompute_job` table under `domain='cold_chain'`, but give it a separate domain-specific lease and worker path; existing dispatch selection/job behavior remains unchanged. Extend `postgres_assessment_store.py` as the sole writer of analytic/assessment and new cold episode tables, and `postgres_finding_store.py` as the sole Finding writer, with a caller-owned transaction spanning both and job completion. The current Finding store hardcodes dispatch mechanism/title/matcher and independently opens transactions: factor domain-specific values and transaction-capable methods rather than calling the dispatch writer for cold. Existing `finding_revision.transition` already permits `SUPPORT_CHANGED`, `BASIS_REFRESHED` and `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED`; preserve that CHECK. `cold_episode.id` is a UUID derived deterministically from the stated SHA-256 episode-key tuple (UUIDv5 under a fixed cold namespace), not the raw SHA text; `anchor_time` is the first qualifying accepted sample/request source time. Add `episode_key_sha256 char(64)` with scoped unique key, replacing the proposed `(site_id,kind,asset_id,anchor_time)` uniqueness because two distinct accepted attempts can share a timestamp. Add nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id` self-FK to `cold_episode` for the promised split relation, with cycle/self checks. The first crossing is anchored by its **accepted evidence UUID**, while a request uses its accepted event UUID; a sample has no event ID. A late record changes revisions and bounds, not episode identity, unless the anchor is invalidated. A `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` FindingRevision retains the old Finding as history; it never deletes it. `finding_revision` has NOT NULL analytic/run/assessment/basis fields: every cold FindingRevision must cite the selected cold analytic run and assessment revision and compute the existing four SHA fields from exact accepted IDs, including canonical `[]` for empty support. The current Finding read/index filters and lineage must be widened by domain/analytic; keep mini-grid filters unchanged. Rule 8 must add all three cold episode tables to `$assessTables` **before** `$assessInserts` is derived, and only the assessment adapter may insert them; test foreign-writer and mutation refusals. This is W2-03 work, not an implicit consequence-writer task.
 
 Role set `cold-roles-1` is an immutable named version in `sites/binding_roles.py`; register in `ROLE_SET_BY_SITE_TYPE` and `SERVED_ROLE_SETS_BY_SITE_TYPE` (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_roles.py:17-27,117-130`). The values below are canonical. `ONE` means one binding per Site, `PER_ASSET` one per named asset. All source/device/signal rows must be registered and linked to the same Site and effective Foundation revision. No binding may silently cover two incompatible roles. Numeric normalization is IDENTITY in v1; state ENUM needs an explicit finite mapping; Celsius conversion is not implicit.

+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` or no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. This is contact, not equipment health. `bindings[].cadence` is the declared cadence of the frozen cold publication profile, `300` seconds for this profile, exposed as `cadence_seconds`; it is neither inferred from readings nor stored on a binding. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.
+
+**W2-02 channel closure.** Add `grid_power_event` / METER, EVENT `POWER_LOSS|POWER_RESTORED`, ONE, `event` unit. Replace the two impossible `backup_request` and `backup_result` channel bindings with **one** required `backup_start_event` / GENERATOR, EVENT `START_REQUEST|START_RESULT`, ONE; require both kinds in capability/readiness, while one registered `(generator-controller,start-event)` channel carries both. `grid_state` remains its separate `(grid-meter,state)` channel. `transfer_result`, `door_event` and `manual_temperature` keep separate channels. The W2-00 closed v4 table and W2-01 snapshot rows must name these exact roles; no channel may be bound twice. On cold panels replace status labels `Mapped`/`Not mapped` with `Configured`/`Needs a source`; use `Reporting`/`No report received` only when an actual selected evidence/contact read proves it. Keep panel titles fixed by the rendered-panel manifest.
+
+**W2-02 code seam closure.** Add cold model and publication identities to `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` `MODEL_PROFILES`/`PUBLICATION_PROFILES`, `host/execution_adapter.py` `EXECUTABLE_MODELS`, `host/lab_execution.py` model/reporting selection and its `_LIFECYCLE` validator, and `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py` `_MAPPING_BY_PUBLICATION_PROFILE`. The latter currently falls back to fuel mapping for an unknown profile: explicitly refuse an unknown cold/EV profile instead of falling back. Add the cold scenario state keys/scopes to the closed state-reference and execution registries and a separate cold pack/observation transform; `kernel/execute.py` currently assumes mini-grid/fuel model laws and does not acquire a new pack merely by receiving a `ModelSpec`. Use a typed pack dispatch boundary or extend generic laws with proof that existing mini-grid vectors are unchanged. `host/lab_execution.py` currently refuses non-measurement staging as V3; select the v4 stage/Batch V2 path by the frozen cold profile, including stable event IDs and pinned registered bindings, while preserving the V3 path. `profiles.py` owns publication cadence; Foundation declares availability/unit only. Do not coerce event null values to readings. Reuse the existing `recompute_job` table under `domain='cold_chain'`, but give it a separate domain-specific lease and worker path; existing dispatch selection/job behavior remains unchanged. Extend `postgres_assessment_store.py` as the sole writer of analytic/assessment and new cold episode tables, and `postgres_finding_store.py` as the sole Finding writer, with a caller-owned transaction spanning both and job completion. The current Finding store hardcodes dispatch mechanism/title/matcher and independently opens transactions: factor domain-specific values and transaction-capable methods rather than calling the dispatch writer for cold. Add `episode_key_sha256 char(64)` with scoped unique key, replacing the proposed `(site_id,kind,asset_id,anchor_time)` uniqueness because two distinct accepted attempts can share a timestamp. Add nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id` self-FK to `cold_episode` for the promised split relation, with cycle/self checks. A `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` FindingRevision retains the old Finding as history; it never deletes it. `finding_revision` has NOT NULL analytic/run/assessment/basis fields: every cold FindingRevision must cite the selected cold analytic run and assessment revision and compute the existing four SHA fields from exact accepted IDs, including canonical `[]` for empty support. The current Finding read/index filters and lineage must be widened by domain/analytic; keep mini-grid filters unchanged. def _decide_v4(basis: AcceptedAsBasis, ordinal: int, envelope_text: str, digest: str, envelope: SourceEnvelopeV4,
               reject) -> Decision:
    """Ruling 4: the exact closed channel tuple and the pinned row's v4 metadata must agree before acceptance."""
    binding = basis.bindings.get((envelope.device_id, envelope.signal_id))
    if binding is None or binding.source_id != envelope.source_id:
        return _unbound(basis, envelope, reject)
    entry = V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS.get((envelope.device_id, envelope.signal_id))
    pinned = binding.v4_metadata()
    if entry is None or pinned is None or pinned != entry.metadata():
        return reject(
            "MAPPING_INCOMPATIBLE", "mapping",
            f"channel {envelope.device_id}/{envelope.signal_id} has no V4 mapping entry that the closed "
            "simulator-lab-gateway-cold/1 table and the pinned binding row both state identically",
        )
    carried = envelope.measurement or envelope.record_kind
    if envelope.family != entry.family or envelope.measurement != entry.measurement:
        return reject("MAPPING_INCOMPATIBLE", "mapping",
                      f"this channel does not carry {envelope.family} {carried}")
    if envelope.family == "EVENT":
        if envelope.record_kind not in pinned["allowed_record_kinds"]:
            return reject("MAPPING_INCOMPATIBLE", "mapping", f"{carried} is not a record kind of this channel")
        if envelope.outcome not in pinned["allowed_outcomes"].get(envelope.record_kind, []):
            return reject("MAPPING_INCOMPATIBLE", "mapping",
                          f"{envelope.outcome} is not an allowed {carried} outcome on this channel")
        if pinned["requires_correlation"] and envelope.correlation_id is None:
            return reject("CORRELATION_INVALID", "payload.correlation_id", "this channel requires correlation")
        if pinned["requires_session"] and envelope.session_id is None:
            return reject("CORRELATION_INVALID", "payload.session_id", "this channel requires a session ID")
    source_time = parse_instant(envelope.source_time)
    refused = _outside_periods(basis, envelope, source_time, reject)
    if refused is not None:
        return refused
    if envelope.unit is not None and (envelope.unit not in pinned["allowed_units"]
                                      or envelope.unit != binding.source_unit):
        return reject("UNIT_INCOMPATIBLE", "payload.unit",
                      f"{envelope.unit!r} is not this channel's registered unit {binding.source_unit!r}")
    ev_scoped = envelope.record_kind in EV_KINDS or envelope.measurement == "INTERVAL_DELTA"
    if ev_scoped and envelope.connector_id is not None and envelope.connector_id != binding.asset_code:
        return reject("EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH", "payload.connector_id",
                      f"{envelope.connector_id!r} is not the bound connector's public identifier")
    if envelope.family == "EVENT":
        if envelope.value is None:
            return _accepted(basis, ordinal, envelope_text, digest, envelope, binding, None, None, None, source_time)
        normalized, kind, refusal = _normalize(binding, envelope.value)
        if kind != "IDENTITY" or refusal is not None:
            return reject("CONVERSION_UNSUPPORTED", "mapping",
                          f"a quantitative event needs the IDENTITY conversion, not {kind!r}")
        return _accepted(basis, ordinal, envelope_text, digest, envelope, binding, normalized,
                         binding.canonical_unit, None, source_time)
    normalized, kind, refusal = _normalize(binding, envelope.value)
    if refusal == "INVALID_ENUM":
        return reject("INVALID_ENUM", "payload.value", f"{envelope.value!r} is not in the registered ENUM map")
    if refusal is not None:
        return reject("CONVERSION_UNSUPPORTED", "mapping", f"conversion {kind!r} is not applied in this release")
    return _accepted(basis, ordinal, envelope_text, digest, envelope, binding, normalized, binding.canonical_unit,
                     kind, source_time)


def _text(value: datetime | None) -> str:
    return "no end" if value is None else value.isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")


def recompute_window(accepted: Sequence[Accepted]) -> tuple[datetime, datetime] | None:
    """The half-open source-time window an analytic must revisit for these acceptances."""
    if not accepted:
        return None
    starts = [a.interval_start or a.source_time for a in accepted]
    ends = [a.interval_end or (a.source_time + timedelta(microseconds=1)) for a in accepted]
    return min(starts), max(ends)


# --- ledger port -----------------------------------------------------------------


@dataclass(frozen=True)
    def allowed_outcomes(self) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
        return {k: sorted(V4_EVENT_OUTCOMES[k]) for k in self.allowed_record_kinds}

    def metadata(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """The five snapshot binding-row keys this entry requires, in their canonical spelling."""
        return {
            "allowed_record_kinds": sorted(self.allowed_record_kinds),
            "allowed_outcomes": self.allowed_outcomes,
            "requires_correlation": self.requires_correlation,
            "requires_session": self.requires_session,
            "allowed_units": sorted(self.allowed_units),
        }


def _point(unit: str) -> ChannelMapping:
    return ChannelMapping("MEASUREMENT", "STATE_AT_INSTANT", (), False, False, (unit,))


def _events(*kinds: str, session: bool = False, units: tuple[str, ...] = ()) -> ChannelMapping:
    return ChannelMapping("EVENT", None, tuple(kinds), True, session, units)


#: Design section 4: the closed cold mapping plus the two reserved EV tuples. Same signal ID on a different
#: device is not an alias. `simulator-lab-gateway-cold/1`, schema 4.
V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS: Mapping[tuple[str, str], ChannelMapping] = {
    ("chamber-probe", "temperature"): _point("degC"),
    ("ambient-probe", "temperature"): _point("degC"),
    ("grid-meter", "state"): _point(DISCRETE_UNIT),
    ("generator-controller", "state"): _point(DISCRETE_UNIT),
    ("compressor-controller", "state"): _point(DISCRETE_UNIT),
    ("backup-bus-meter", "state"): _point(DISCRETE_UNIT),
    ("compressor-meter", "power"): ChannelMapping("MEASUREMENT", "INTERVAL_MEAN", (), False, False, ("kW",)),
    ("door-sensor", "event"): _events("DOOR_OPEN", "DOOR_CLOSE"),
    ("generator-controller", "start-event"): _events("START_REQUEST", "START_RESULT"),
    ("transfer-controller", "event"): _events("TRANSFER_RESULT"),
    ("grid-meter", "event"): _events("POWER_LOSS", "POWER_RESTORED"),
    ("operator-log", "temperature-event"): _events("MANUAL_LOG", units=(MANUAL_LOG_UNIT,)),
    ("charger-controller", "session-event"): _events("EV_SESSION_START", "EV_SESSION_END", "EV_SESSION_ABORT",
                                                     session=True),
    ("connector-controller", "fault-event"): _events("EV_CONNECTOR_FAULT", "EV_CONNECTOR_CLEAR"),
}

V4_BINDING_METADATA_KEYS = ("allowed_record_kinds", "allowed_outcomes", "requires_correlation",
                            "requires_session", "allowed_units")


def canonical_binding_metadata(row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """A snapshot binding row's five v4 keys in canonical spelling, or None when any is absent or ill-typed."""
    if any(row.get(k) is None for k in V4_BINDING_METADATA_KEYS):
        return None
    kinds, outcomes, units = row["allowed_record_kinds"], row["allowed_outcomes"], row["allowed_units"]
    if (not isinstance(kinds, (list, tuple)) or not all(type(k) is str for k in kinds)
            or not isinstance(units, (list, tuple)) or not all(type(u) is str for u in units)
            or not isinstance(outcomes, Mapping)
            or not all(type(k) is str and isinstance(v, (list, tuple)) and all(type(o) is str for o in v)
    _reject_unknown_keys(
        mapping, TOPOLOGY_NODE_KEYS, where=where, source=source, invalid=invalid
    )

    node_id = _require_identifier(
        mapping, "node_id", where=where, source=source, invalid=invalid
    )
    component_id = _require_component_reference(
        mapping,
        "component_id",
        component_ids=component_ids,
        where=where,
        source=source,
        invalid=invalid,
    )
    node_role = _require_choice(
        mapping,
        "node_role",
        TOPOLOGY_NODE_ROLES,
        where=where,
        source=source,
        invalid=invalid,
    )

    return TopologyNode(
        node_id=node_id, component_id=component_id, node_role=node_role
    )


def _parse_connection(
    raw: Any,
    *,
    node_ids: frozenset[str],
    where: str,
    source: str,
    invalid: Invalid,
) -> TopologyConnection:
    mapping = _require_mapping(raw, where=where, source=source, invalid=invalid)
    _reject_unknown_keys(
        mapping, CONNECTION_KEYS, where=where, source=source, invalid=invalid
    )

    connection_id = _require_identifier(
        mapping, "connection_id", where=where, source=source, invalid=invalid
    )
    from_node = _require_node_reference(
        mapping, "from_node", node_ids=node_ids, where=where, source=source, invalid=invalid
    )
    to_node = _require_node_reference(
        mapping, "to_node", node_ids=node_ids, where=where, source=source, invalid=invalid
    )

    if from_node == to_node:
        invalid(
            f"'{where}' connects {from_node!r} to itself in {source}. A "
            "connection joins two topology nodes."
        )

    medium = _require_choice(
        mapping, "medium", CONNECTION_MEDIA, where=where, source=source, invalid=invalid
    )

    return TopologyConnection(
        connection_id=connection_id,
        from_node=from_node,
        to_node=to_node,
        medium=medium,
    )

+## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` or no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. This is contact, not equipment health. `bindings[].cadence` is the declared cadence of the frozen cold publication profile, `300` seconds for this profile, exposed as `cadence_seconds`; it is neither inferred from readings nor stored on a binding. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.
+

Replace the two impossible `backup_request` and `backup_result` channel bindings with **one** required `backup_start_event` / GENERATOR, EVENT `START_REQUEST|START_RESULT`, ONE; require both kinds in capability/readiness, while one registered `(generator-controller,start-event)` channel carries both. `grid_state` remains its separate `(grid-meter,state)` channel. `transfer_result`, `door_event` and `manual_temperature` keep separate channels. Do not coerce event null values to readings. Reuse the existing `recompute_job` table under `domain='cold_chain'`, but give it a separate domain-specific lease and worker path; existing dispatch selection/job behavior remains unchanged. Extend `postgres_assessment_store.py` as the sole writer of analytic/assessment and new cold episode tables, and `postgres_finding_store.py` as the sole Finding writer, with a caller-owned transaction spanning both and job completion. The current Finding store hardcodes dispatch mechanism/title/matcher and independently opens transactions: factor domain-specific values and transaction-capable methods rather than calling the dispatch writer for cold. Add `episode_key_sha256 char(64)` with scoped unique key, replacing the proposed `(site_id,kind,asset_id,anchor_time)` uniqueness because two distinct accepted attempts can share a timestamp. Add nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id` self-FK to `cold_episode` for the promised split relation, with cycle/self checks. The first crossing is anchored by its **accepted evidence UUID**, while a request uses its accepted event UUID; a sample has no event ID. A late record changes revisions and bounds, not episode identity, unless the anchor is invalidated. A `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` FindingRevision retains the old Finding as history; it never deletes it. `finding_revision` has NOT NULL analytic/run/assessment/basis fields: every cold FindingRevision must cite the selected cold analytic run and assessment revision and compute the existing four SHA fields from exact accepted IDs, including canonical `[]` for empty support. The current Finding read/index filters and lineage must be widened by domain/analytic; keep mini-grid filters unchanged.
