-## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` or no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. This is contact, not equipment health. `bindings[].cadence` is the declared cadence of the frozen cold publication profile, `300` seconds for this profile, exposed as `cadence_seconds`; it is neither inferred from readings nor stored on a binding. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.
+
+**W2-02 channel closure.** Add `grid_power_event` / METER, EVENT `POWER_LOSS|POWER_RESTORED`, ONE, `event` unit. Replace the two impossible `backup_request` and `backup_result` channel bindings with **one** required `backup_start_event` / GENERATOR, EVENT `START_REQUEST|START_RESULT`, ONE; require both kinds in capability/readiness, while one registered `(generator-controller,start-event)` channel carries both. `grid_state` remains its separate `(grid-meter,state)` channel. `transfer_result`, `door_event` and `manual_temperature` keep separate channels. The W2-00 closed v4 table and W2-01 snapshot rows must name these exact roles; no channel may be bound twice. On cold panels replace status labels `Mapped`/`Not mapped` with `Configured`/`Needs a source`; use `Reporting`/`No report received` only when an actual selected evidence/contact read proves it. Keep panel titles fixed by the rendered-panel manifest.
+
+**W2-02 code seam closure.** Add cold model and publication identities to `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` `MODEL_PROFILES`/`PUBLICATION_PROFILES`, `host/execution_adapter.py` `EXECUTABLE_MODELS`, `host/lab_execution.py` model/reporting selection and its `_LIFECYCLE` validator, and `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py` `_MAPPING_BY_PUBLICATION_PROFILE`. The latter currently falls back to fuel mapping for an unknown profile: explicitly refuse an unknown cold/EV profile instead of falling back. Add the cold scenario state keys/scopes to the closed state-reference and execution registries and a separate cold pack/observation transform; `kernel/execute.py` currently assumes mini-grid/fuel model laws and does not acquire a new pack merely by receiving a `ModelSpec`. Introduce a typed pack dispatch boundary selected by the frozen `model_profile_id/version`; execute `cold-thermal/1` through that boundary, with existing mini-grid/fuel paths and golden vectors unchanged. `host/lab_execution.py` currently refuses non-measurement staging as V3; select the v4 stage/Batch V2 path by the frozen cold profile, including stable event IDs and pinned registered bindings, while preserving the V3 path. `profiles.py` owns publication cadence; Foundation declares availability/unit only. Freeze observation/profile/pack identity before Draft and include them in run identity. W2-02 may not call S2 with a synthetic unregistered mapping or use the W2-00 test-only injected basis.
+
+**W2-03 code seam closure.** The current `assessment_worker.py` is dispatch-only and `postgres_assessment_store.py` selects numeric readings; add a cold-specific typed evidence query/worker and adapter methods that select both v4 measurement and EVENT rows by accepted disposition decision time, tenant/Site, role, selected Foundation/binding and half-open source interval. Do not coerce event null values to readings. Reuse the existing `recompute_job` table under `domain='cold_chain'`, but give it a separate domain-specific lease and worker path; existing dispatch selection/job behavior remains unchanged. Extend `postgres_assessment_store.py` as the sole writer of analytic/assessment and new cold episode tables, and `postgres_finding_store.py` as the sole Finding writer, with a caller-owned transaction spanning both and job completion. The current Finding store hardcodes dispatch mechanism/title/matcher and independently opens transactions: factor domain-specific values and transaction-capable methods rather than calling the dispatch writer for cold. Existing `finding_revision.transition` already permits `SUPPORT_CHANGED`, `BASIS_REFRESHED` and `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED`; preserve that CHECK. `cold_episode.id` is a UUID derived deterministically from the stated SHA-256 episode-key tuple (UUIDv5 under a fixed cold namespace), not the raw SHA text; `anchor_time` is the first qualifying accepted sample/request source time. Add `episode_key_sha256 char(64)` with scoped unique key, replacing the proposed `(site_id,kind,asset_id,anchor_time)` uniqueness because two distinct accepted attempts can share a timestamp. Add nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id` self-FK to `cold_episode` for the promised split relation, with cycle/self checks. The first crossing is anchored by its **accepted evidence UUID**, while a request uses its accepted event UUID; a sample has no event ID. A late record changes revisions and bounds, not episode identity, unless the anchor is invalidated. A `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` FindingRevision retains the old Finding as history; it never deletes it. `finding_revision` has NOT NULL analytic/run/assessment/basis fields: every cold FindingRevision must cite the selected cold analytic run and assessment revision and compute the existing four SHA fields from exact accepted IDs, including canonical `[]` for empty support. The current Finding read/index filters and lineage must be widened by domain/analytic; keep mini-grid filters unchanged. Rule 8 must add all three cold episode tables to `$assessTables` **before** `$assessInserts` is derived, and only the assessment adapter may insert them; test foreign-writer and mutation refusals. This is W2-03 work, not an implicit consequence-writer task.
 
 Role set `cold-roles-1` is an immutable named version in `sites/binding_roles.py`; register in `ROLE_SET_BY_SITE_TYPE` and `SERVED_ROLE_SETS_BY_SITE_TYPE` (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_roles.py:17-27,117-130`). The values below are canonical. `ONE` means one binding per Site, `PER_ASSET` one per named asset. All source/device/signal rows must be registered and linked to the same Site and effective Foundation revision. No binding may silently cover two incompatible roles. Numeric normalization is IDENTITY in v1; state ENUM needs an explicit finite mapping; Celsius conversion is not implicit.

+## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+

This is contact, not equipment health. `bindings[].cadence` is the declared cadence of the frozen cold publication profile, `300` seconds for this profile, exposed as `cadence_seconds`; it is neither inferred from readings nor stored on a binding. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.
+

+## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+Migration table correction under `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers`: `0021` also adds the thirteenth `cold-compressor-start-delay` property and exact carriers, units and bounds below. `0022` adds `cold_episode.episode_key_sha256` with unique `(tenant_id,site_id,episode_key_sha256)` and nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id`; remove the proposed unique `(site_id,kind,asset_id,anchor_time)` because distinct accepted requests can share source time. The existing `0009` Finding transition CHECK already admits the three cold transitions and stays intact. These corrections supersede the shorter migration rows above.
+
+### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` or no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. This is contact, not equipment health. `bindings[].cadence` is the declared cadence of the frozen cold publication profile, `300` seconds for this profile, exposed as `cadence_seconds`; it is neither inferred from readings nor stored on a binding. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.
+
+**W2-02 channel closure.** Add `grid_power_event` / METER, EVENT `POWER_LOSS|POWER_RESTORED`, ONE, `event` unit. Replace the two impossible `backup_request` and `backup_result` channel bindings with **one** required `backup_start_event` / GENERATOR, EVENT `START_REQUEST|START_RESULT`, ONE; require both kinds in capability/readiness, while one registered `(generator-controller,start-event)` channel carries both. `grid_state` remains its separate `(grid-meter,state)` channel. `transfer_result`, `door_event` and `manual_temperature` keep separate channels. The W2-00 closed v4 table and W2-01 snapshot rows must name these exact roles; no channel may be bound twice. On cold panels replace status labels `Mapped`/`Not mapped` with `Configured`/`Needs a source`; use `Reporting`/`No report received` only when an actual selected evidence/contact read proves it. Keep panel titles fixed by the rendered-panel manifest.
+
+**W2-02 code seam closure.** Add cold model and publication identities to `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` `MODEL_PROFILES`/`PUBLICATION_PROFILES`, `host/execution_adapter.py` `EXECUTABLE_MODELS`, `host/lab_execution.py` model/reporting selection and its `_LIFECYCLE` validator, and `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py` `_MAPPING_BY_PUBLICATION_PROFILE`. The latter currently falls back to fuel mapping for an unknown profile: explicitly refuse an unknown cold/EV profile instead of falling back. Add the cold scenario state keys/scopes to the closed state-reference and execution registries and a separate cold pack/observation transform; `kernel/execute.py` currently assumes mini-grid/fuel model laws and does not acquire a new pack merely by receiving a `ModelSpec`. Introduce a typed pack dispatch boundary selected by the frozen `model_profile_id/version`; execute `cold-thermal/1` through that boundary, with existing mini-grid/fuel paths and golden vectors unchanged. `host/lab_execution.py` currently refuses non-measurement staging as V3; select the v4 stage/Batch V2 path by the frozen cold profile, including stable event IDs and pinned registered bindings, while preserving the V3 path. `profiles.py` owns publication cadence; Foundation declares availability/unit only. Freeze observation/profile/pack identity before Draft and include them in run identity. W2-02 may not call S2 with a synthetic unregistered mapping or use the W2-00 test-only injected basis.
+
+**W2-03 code seam closure.** The current `assessment_worker.py` is dispatch-only and `postgres_assessment_store.py` selects numeric readings; add a cold-specific typed evidence query/worker and adapter methods that select both v4 measurement and EVENT rows by accepted disposition decision time, tenant/Site, role, selected Foundation/binding and half-open source interval. Do not coerce event null values to readings. Reuse the existing `recompute_job` table under `domain='cold_chain'`, but give it a separate domain-specific lease and worker path; existing dispatch selection/job behavior remains unchanged. Extend `postgres_assessment_store.py` as the sole writer of analytic/assessment and new cold episode tables, and `postgres_finding_store.py` as the sole Finding writer, with a caller-owned transaction spanning both and job completion. The current Finding store hardcodes dispatch mechanism/title/matcher and independently opens transactions: factor domain-specific values and transaction-capable methods rather than calling the dispatch writer for cold. Existing `finding_revision.transition` already permits `SUPPORT_CHANGED`, `BASIS_REFRESHED` and `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED`; preserve that CHECK. `cold_episode.id` is a UUID derived deterministically from the stated SHA-256 episode-key tuple (UUIDv5 under a fixed cold namespace), not the raw SHA text; `anchor_time` is the first qualifying accepted sample/request source time. Add `episode_key_sha256 char(64)` with scoped unique key, replacing the proposed `(site_id,kind,asset_id,anchor_time)` uniqueness because two distinct accepted attempts can share a timestamp. Add nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id` self-FK to `cold_episode` for the promised split relation, with cycle/self checks. The first crossing is anchored by its **accepted evidence UUID**, while a request uses its accepted event UUID; a sample has no event ID. A late record changes revisions and bounds, not episode identity, unless the anchor is invalidated. A `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` FindingRevision retains the old Finding as history; it never deletes it. `finding_revision` has NOT NULL analytic/run/assessment/basis fields: every cold FindingRevision must cite the selected cold analytic run and assessment revision and compute the existing four SHA fields from exact accepted IDs, including canonical `[]` for empty support. +## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+Migration table correction under `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers`: `0021` also adds the thirteenth `cold-compressor-start-delay` property and exact carriers, units and bounds below. `0022` adds `cold_episode.episode_key_sha256` with unique `(tenant_id,site_id,episode_key_sha256)` and nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id`; remove the proposed unique `(site_id,kind,asset_id,anchor_time)` because distinct accepted requests can share source time. The existing `0009` Finding transition CHECK already admits the three cold transitions and stays intact. These corrections supersede the shorter migration rows above.
+
+### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` or no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. This is contact, not equipment health. `bindings[].cadence` is the declared cadence of the frozen cold publication profile, `300` seconds for this profile, exposed as `cadence_seconds`; it is neither inferred from readings nor stored on a binding. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.
+
+**W2-02 channel closure.** Add `grid_power_event` / METER, EVENT `POWER_LOSS|POWER_RESTORED`, ONE, `event` unit. Replace the two impossible `backup_request` and `backup_result` channel bindings with **one** required `backup_start_event` / GENERATOR, EVENT `START_REQUEST|START_RESULT`, ONE; require both kinds in capability/readiness, while one registered `(generator-controller,start-event)` channel carries both. `grid_state` remains its separate `(grid-meter,state)` channel. `transfer_result`, `door_event` and `manual_temperature` keep separate channels. The W2-00 closed v4 table and W2-01 snapshot rows must name these exact roles; no channel may be bound twice. On cold panels replace status labels `Mapped`/`Not mapped` with `Configured`/`Needs a source`; use `Reporting`/`No report received` only when an actual selected evidence/contact read proves it. Keep panel titles fixed by the rendered-panel manifest.
+
+**W2-02 code seam closure.** Add cold model and publication identities to `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` `MODEL_PROFILES`/`PUBLICATION_PROFILES`, `host/execution_adapter.py` `EXECUTABLE_MODELS`, `host/lab_execution.py` model/reporting selection and its `_LIFECYCLE` validator, and `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py` `_MAPPING_BY_PUBLICATION_PROFILE`. The latter currently falls back to fuel mapping for an unknown profile: explicitly refuse an unknown cold/EV profile instead of falling back. Add the cold scenario state keys/scopes to the closed state-reference and execution registries and a separate cold pack/observation transform; `kernel/execute.py` currently assumes mini-grid/fuel model laws and does not acquire a new pack merely by receiving a `ModelSpec`. Introduce a typed pack dispatch boundary selected by the frozen `model_profile_id/version`; execute `cold-thermal/1` through that boundary, with existing mini-grid/fuel paths and golden vectors unchanged. `host/lab_execution.py` currently refuses non-measurement staging as V3; select the v4 stage/Batch V2 path by the frozen cold profile, including stable event IDs and pinned registered bindings, while preserving the V3 path. `profiles.py` owns publication cadence; Foundation declares availability/unit only. Freeze observation/profile/pack identity before Draft and include them in run identity. W2-02 may not call S2 with a synthetic unregistered mapping or use the W2-00 test-only injected basis.
+
+**W2-03 code seam closure.** The current `assessment_worker.py` is dispatch-only and `postgres_assessment_store.py` selects numeric readings; add a cold-specific typed evidence query/worker and adapter methods that select both v4 measurement and EVENT rows by accepted disposition decision time, tenant/Site, role, selected Foundation/binding and half-open source interval. Do not coerce event null values to readings. Reuse the existing `recompute_job` table under `domain='cold_chain'`, but give it a separate domain-specific lease and worker path; existing dispatch selection/job behavior remains unchanged. Extend `postgres_assessment_store.py` as the sole writer of analytic/assessment and new cold episode tables, and `postgres_finding_store.py` as the sole Finding writer, with a caller-owned transaction spanning both and job completion. The current Finding store hardcodes dispatch mechanism/title/matcher and independently opens transactions: factor domain-specific values and transaction-capable methods rather than calling the dispatch writer for cold. Existing `finding_revision.transition` already permits `SUPPORT_CHANGED`, `BASIS_REFRESHED` and `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED`; preserve that CHECK. `cold_episode.id` is a UUID derived deterministically from the stated SHA-256 episode-key tuple (UUIDv5 under a fixed cold namespace), not the raw SHA text; `anchor_time` is the first qualifying accepted sample/request source time. Add `episode_key_sha256 char(64)` with scoped unique key, replacing the proposed `(site_id,kind,asset_id,anchor_time)` uniqueness because two distinct accepted attempts can share a timestamp. Add nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id` self-FK to `cold_episode` for the promised split relation, with cycle/self checks. The first crossing is anchored by its **accepted evidence UUID**, while a request uses its accepted event UUID; a sample has no event ID. A late record changes revisions and bounds, not episode identity, unless the anchor is invalidated. A `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` FindingRevision retains the old Finding as history; it never deletes it. `finding_revision` has NOT NULL analytic/run/assessment/basis fields: every cold FindingRevision must cite the selected cold analytic run and assessment revision and compute the existing four SHA fields from exact accepted IDs, including canonical `[]` for empty support. The current Finding read/index filters and lineage must be widened by domain/analytic; keep mini-grid filters unchanged. Rule 8 must add all three cold episode tables to `$assessTables` **before** `$assessInserts` is derived, and only the assessment adapter may insert them; test foreign-writer and mutation refusals. This is W2-03 work, not an implicit consequence-writer task.
 
 Role set `cold-roles-1` is an immutable named version in `sites/binding_roles.py`; register in `ROLE_SET_BY_SITE_TYPE` and `SERVED_ROLE_SETS_BY_SITE_TYPE` (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_roles.py:17-27,117-130`). The values below are canonical. `ONE` means one binding per Site, `PER_ASSET` one per named asset. All source/device/signal rows must be registered and linked to the same Site and effective Foundation revision. No binding may silently cover two incompatible roles. Numeric normalization is IDENTITY in v1; state ENUM needs an explicit finite mapping; Celsius conversion is not implicit.

+## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+Migration table correction under `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers`: `0021` also adds the thirteenth `cold-compressor-start-delay` property and exact carriers, units and bounds below. `0022` adds `cold_episode.episode_key_sha256` with unique `(tenant_id,site_id,episode_key_sha256)` and nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id`; remove the proposed unique `(site_id,kind,asset_id,anchor_time)` because distinct accepted requests can share source time. The existing `0009` Finding transition CHECK already admits the three cold transitions and stays intact. These corrections supersede the shorter migration rows above.
+
+### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` with no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. If a diagnostic exists but its freshness is UNKNOWN, report `STALE` with limitation `Contact time cannot be verified`, never claim recent contact. This is contact, not equipment health. W2-01 publishes `bindings[].cadence_seconds=300` as the fixed `cold-roles-1` channel contract, not from stored binding rows or readings; W2-02's frozen `cold-publication/1` profile must declare the same 300-second sampling cadence and refuse a mismatch. Event channels are transition-driven and return `cadence_seconds=null`, with a `transition` publication mode. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.
+
+**W2-02 channel closure.** Add `grid_power_event` / METER, EVENT `POWER_LOSS|POWER_RESTORED`, ONE, `event` unit. Replace the two impossible `backup_request` and `backup_result` channel bindings with **one** required `backup_start_event` / GENERATOR, EVENT `START_REQUEST|START_RESULT`, ONE; require both kinds in capability/readiness, while one registered `(generator-controller,start-event)` channel carries both. `grid_state` remains its separate `(grid-meter,state)` channel. `transfer_result`, `door_event` and `manual_temperature` keep separate channels. The W2-00 closed v4 table and W2-01 snapshot rows must name these exact roles; no channel may be bound twice. On cold panels replace status labels `Mapped`/`Not mapped` with `Configured`/`Needs a source`; use `Reporting`/`No report received` only when an actual selected evidence/contact read proves it. Keep panel titles fixed by the rendered-panel manifest.
+
+**W2-02 code seam closure.** Add cold model and publication identities to `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` `MODEL_PROFILES`/`PUBLICATION_PROFILES`, `host/execution_adapter.py` `EXECUTABLE_MODELS`, `host/lab_execution.py` model/reporting selection and its `_LIFECYCLE` validator, and `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py` `_MAPPING_BY_PUBLICATION_PROFILE`. The latter currently falls back to fuel mapping for an unknown profile: explicitly refuse an unknown cold/EV profile instead of falling back. Add the cold scenario state keys/scopes to the closed state-reference and execution registries and a separate cold pack/observation transform; `kernel/execute.py` currently assumes mini-grid/fuel model laws and does not acquire a new pack merely by receiving a `ModelSpec`. Introduce a typed pack dispatch boundary selected by the frozen `model_profile_id/version`; execute `cold-thermal/1` through that boundary, with existing mini-grid/fuel paths and golden vectors unchanged. `host/lab_execution.py` currently refuses non-measurement staging as V3; select the v4 stage/Batch V2 path by the frozen cold profile, including stable event IDs and pinned registered bindings, while preserving the V3 path. `profiles.py` owns publication cadence; Foundation declares availability/unit only. Freeze observation/profile/pack identity before Draft and include them in run identity. W2-02 may not call S2 with a synthetic unregistered mapping or use the W2-00 test-only injected basis.
+
+**W2-03 code seam closure.** The current `assessment_worker.py` is dispatch-only and `postgres_assessment_store.py` selects numeric readings; add a cold-specific typed evidence query/worker and adapter methods that select both v4 measurement and EVENT rows by accepted disposition decision time, tenant/Site, role, selected Foundation/binding and half-open source interval. Do not coerce event null values to readings. Reuse the existing `recompute_job` table under `domain='cold_chain'`, but give it a separate domain-specific lease and worker path; existing dispatch selection/job behavior remains unchanged. Extend `postgres_assessment_store.py` as the sole writer of analytic/assessment and new cold episode tables, and `postgres_finding_store.py` as the sole Finding writer, with a caller-owned transaction spanning both and job completion. The current Finding store hardcodes dispatch mechanism/title/matcher and independently opens transactions: factor domain-specific values and transaction-capable methods rather than calling the dispatch writer for cold. Existing `finding_revision.transition` already permits `SUPPORT_CHANGED`, `BASIS_REFRESHED` and `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED`; preserve that CHECK. `cold_episode.id` is a UUID derived deterministically from the stated SHA-256 episode-key tuple (UUIDv5 under a fixed cold namespace), not the raw SHA text; `anchor_time` is the first qualifying accepted sample/request source time. Add `episode_key_sha256 char(64)` with scoped unique key, replacing the proposed `(site_id,kind,asset_id,anchor_time)` uniqueness because two distinct accepted attempts can share a timestamp. Add nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id` self-FK to `cold_episode` for the promised split relation, with cycle/self checks. The first crossing is anchored by its **accepted evidence UUID**, while a request uses its accepted event UUID; a sample has no event ID. A late record changes revisions and bounds, not episode identity, unless the anchor is invalidated. A `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` FindingRevision retains the old Finding as history; it never deletes it. `finding_revision` has NOT NULL analytic/run/assessment/basis fields: every cold FindingRevision must cite the selected cold analytic run and assessment revision and compute the existing four SHA fields from exact accepted IDs, including canonical `[]` for empty support. The current Finding read/index filters and lineage must be widened by domain/analytic; keep mini-grid filters unchanged. Rule 8 must add all three cold episode tables to `$assessTables` **before** `$assessInserts` is derived, and only the assessment adapter may insert them; test foreign-writer and mutation refusals. This is W2-03 work, not an implicit consequence-writer task.
 
 Role set `cold-roles-1` is an immutable named version in `sites/binding_roles.py`; register in `ROLE_SET_BY_SITE_TYPE` and `SERVED_ROLE_SETS_BY_SITE_TYPE` (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_roles.py:17-27,117-130`). The values below are canonical. `ONE` means one binding per Site, `PER_ASSET` one per named asset. All source/device/signal rows must be registered and linked to the same Site and effective Foundation revision. No binding may silently cover two incompatible roles. Numeric normalization is IDENTITY in v1; state ENUM needs an explicit finite mapping; Celsius conversion is not implicit.

Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` with no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. If a diagnostic exists but its freshness is UNKNOWN, report `STALE` with limitation `Contact time cannot be verified`, never claim recent contact. This is contact, not equipment health. W2-01 publishes `bindings[].cadence_seconds=300` as the fixed `cold-roles-1` channel contract, not from stored binding rows or readings; W2-02's frozen `cold-publication/1` profile must declare the same 300-second sampling cadence and refuse a mismatch. Event channels are transition-driven and return `cadence_seconds=null`, with a `transition` publication mode. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.

Do not coerce event null values to readings. Extend `postgres_assessment_store.py` as the sole writer of analytic/assessment and new cold episode tables, and `postgres_finding_store.py` as the sole Finding writer, with a caller-owned transaction spanning both and job completion. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.

+**W2-03 code seam closure.** The current `assessment_worker.py` is dispatch-only and `postgres_assessment_store.py` selects numeric readings; add a cold-specific typed evidence query/worker and adapter methods that select both v4 measurement and EVENT rows by accepted disposition decision time, tenant/Site, role, selected Foundation/binding and half-open source interval. Extend `postgres_assessment_store.py` as the sole writer of analytic/assessment and new cold episode tables, and `postgres_finding_store.py` as the sole Finding writer, with a caller-owned transaction spanning both and job completion. The current Finding store hardcodes dispatch mechanism/title/matcher and independently opens transactions: factor domain-specific values and transaction-capable methods rather than calling the dispatch writer for cold. Existing `finding_revision.transition` already permits `SUPPORT_CHANGED`, `BASIS_REFRESHED` and `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED`; preserve that CHECK. `cold_episode.id` is a UUID derived deterministically from the stated SHA-256 episode-key tuple (UUIDv5 under a fixed cold namespace), not the raw SHA text; `anchor_time` is the first qualifying accepted sample/request source time. +## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+Migration table correction under `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers`: `0021` also adds the thirteenth `cold-compressor-start-delay` property and exact carriers, units and bounds below. `0022` adds `cold_episode.episode_key_sha256` with unique `(tenant_id,site_id,episode_key_sha256)` and nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id`; remove the proposed unique `(site_id,kind,asset_id,anchor_time)` because distinct accepted requests can share source time. +### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` with no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. If a diagnostic exists but its freshness is UNKNOWN, report `STALE` with limitation `Contact time cannot be verified`, never claim recent contact. This is contact, not equipment health. W2-01 publishes `bindings[].cadence_seconds=300` as the fixed `cold-roles-1` channel contract, not from stored binding rows or readings; W2-02's frozen `cold-publication/1` profile must declare the same 300-second sampling cadence and refuse a mismatch. Event channels are transition-driven and return `cadence_seconds=null`, with a `transition` publication mode. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status · {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} · {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.

+## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+Migration table correction under `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers`: `0021` also adds the thirteenth `cold-compressor-start-delay` property and exact carriers, units and bounds below. `0022` adds `cold_episode.episode_key_sha256` with unique `(tenant_id,site_id,episode_key_sha256)` and nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id`; remove the proposed unique `(site_id,kind,asset_id,anchor_time)` because distinct accepted requests can share source time. +### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.
+
+**D–I — selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` with no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. If a diagnostic exists but its freshness is UNKNOWN, report `STALE` with limitation `Contact time cannot be verified`, never claim recent contact. This is contact, not equipment health. Event channels are transition-driven and return `cadence_seconds=null`, with a `transition` publication mode. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. +## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+Migration table correction under `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers`: `0021` also adds the thirteenth `cold-compressor-start-delay` property and exact carriers, units and bounds below. `0022` adds `cold_episode.episode_key_sha256` with unique `(tenant_id,site_id,episode_key_sha256)` and nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id`; remove the proposed unique `(site_id,kind,asset_id,anchor_time)` because distinct accepted requests can share source time. +### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)
+
+The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.
+
+**A — topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.
+
+**B — event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.
+
+**C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.

Add nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id` self-FK to `cold_episode` for the promised split relation, with cycle/self checks. The first crossing is anchored by its **accepted evidence UUID**, while a request uses its accepted event UUID; a sample has no event ID. A late record changes revisions and bounds, not episode identity, unless the anchor is invalidated. A `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` FindingRevision retains the old Finding as history; it never deletes it. `finding_revision` has NOT NULL analytic/run/assessment/basis fields: every cold FindingRevision cites the selected cold analytic run and assessment revision; `support_sha256` and `cited_evidence_sha256` hash their exact accepted ID/envelope-SHA pairs (canonical `[]` when empty), `input_evidence_sha256` copies the analytic run input digest, and `basis_sha256` hashes the full revision basis. The current Finding read/index filters and lineage must be widened by domain/analytic; keep mini-grid filters unchanged. Rule 8 must add all three cold episode tables to `$assessTables` **before** `$assessInserts` is derived, and only the assessment adapter may insert them; test foreign-writer and mutation refusals. This is W2-03 work, not an implicit consequence-writer task.
 
 Role set `cold-roles-1` is an immutable named version in `sites/binding_roles.py`; register in `ROLE_SET_BY_SITE_TYPE` and `SERVED_ROLE_SETS_BY_SITE_TYPE` (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_roles.py:17-27,117-130`). The values below are canonical. `ONE` means one binding per Site, `PER_ASSET` one per named asset. All source/device/signal rows must be registered and linked to the same Site and effective Foundation revision. No binding may silently cover two incompatible roles. Numeric normalization is IDENTITY in v1; state ENUM needs an explicit finite mapping; Celsius conversion is not implicit.

+## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract
+
+Migration table correction under `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers`: `0021` also adds the thirteenth `cold-compressor-start-delay` property and exact carriers, units and bounds below. `0022` adds `cold_episode.episode_key_sha256` with unique `(tenant_id,site_id,episode_key_sha256)` and nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id`; remove the proposed unique `(site_id,kind,asset_id,anchor_time)` because distinct accepted requests can share source time.
